SAṂYUTTA-NIKĀYA.

DIVISION V. MAHĀVAGGO.
Namo tassa bhagavato arahato sammāsaṃbuddhassa ║ ║.

BOOK I.
MAGGA-SAṂYUTTAM XLV.

CHAPTER I. AVIJJĀVAGGO PATHAMO.

(1) Avijjā.
1. Evam me sutaṃ ekaṃ samayam Bhagavā Sāvatthiyaṃ
viharati Jetavane Anāthapindikassa ārāme ║ ║
2. Tatra kho Bhagavā bhikkhū āmantesi Bhikkhavo ti ║ ║
Bhadante ti te bhikkhū Bhagavato paccassosuṃ ║ ║
Bhagavā etad avoca ║ ║
3. Avijjā bhikkhave pubbaṅgamā akusalānaṃ dhammā-
nam samāpattiyā1 anudeva2 ahirikam anottappaṃ ║ ║ Avij-
jāgatassa bhikkhave aviddasuno micchādiṭṭhi pahoti ║ mic-
chādiṭṭhissa micchāsaṅkappo pahoti ║ micchāsaṅkappassa
micchāvācā pahoti ║ micchāvācassa micchākammanto pa-
hoti ║ micchākammantassa micchāājīvo pahoti ║ micchāājī-
vassa micchāvāyāmo pahoti ║ micchāvāyāmassa micchāsati
pahoti ║ micchāsatissa micchāsamādhi pahoti ║ ║
4. Vijjā ca kho bhikkhave pubbaṅgamā kusalānaṃ dham-
mānaṃ samāpattiyā3 anudeva hirottappaṃ ║ ║ Vijjāgatassa

kami ║ upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantam abhivādetvā ekam an-

hidam Ānanda brahmacariyaṃ yad idaṃ kalyāṇamittatā
kalyāṇasahāyatā kalyāṇasampavaṅkatā ║ ║ Kalyāṇamittas-
setam Ānanda bhikkhuno pāṭikaṅkham3 kalyāṇasahāyassa

kalyāṇasampavaṅko ariyam atthaṅgikam maggam bahulī-

kanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ nirodhanissitaṃ vossaggapariṇā-
miṃ ║ sammāsaṅkappam bhāveti vivekanissitam ║ la ║ sammā-
vācam bhāveti ║ sammākammantam bhāveti ║ sammāājīvam
bhāveti ║ sammāvāyāmam bhāveti ║ sammāsatim bhāveti ║
sammāsamādhim bhāveti vivekanissitam virāganissitam ni-
rodhanissitam vossaggapariṇāmiṃ ║ ║ Evaṃ kho Ānanda

ariyam aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggam bhāveti ariyam aṭṭhaṅgikam

which run on according to the pattern of I, -- are to be found

abridgments, but altogether omitted in the Singhalese Mss which
merely alluded to them by saying: Vārasampekhenalikhitāṃ peyyālo

CHAPTER II. PĀSĀDAKAMPANA-VAGGO DUTIYO.

(1) Pubbe or Hetu.
1. Sāvatthi ║ ║
2. Pubbeva13 me bhikkhave sambodhāya anabhisam-

CHAPTER X. BAHUTARĀ SATTĀ.

(1) Khetta.
4. Evam eva kho bhikkhave appakā te sattā ye khetta-
vatthupaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratā ║ atha kho ete va bahutarā
sattā ye khettavatthupaṭiggahanā appaṭiviratā ║ la ║ ║

(2) Kayavikkaya.
4. Evam eva kho bhikkhave appakā te sattā ye kaya-
vikkayā paṭiviratā ║ atha kho ete va bahutarā sattā ye
kayavikkayā1 appaṭivaratā ║ la ║ ║

(3) Dūteyyam.
4. Evam eva kho bhikkhave appakā te sattā ye dūteyya-
pahīnagamanānuyogā paṭiviratā ║ atha kho ete va bahutarā
sattā ye dūteyyapahīnagamanānuyogā appaṭiviratā ║ la ║ ║

(4) Tulākūṭam.
4. Evam eva kho bhikkhave appakā te sattā ye tulā-
kūṭa-{kaṃsakūṭa}-mānakūṭā2 pativiratā ║ atha kho ete va
bahutarā sattā ye tulākūṭa-kaṃsakūṭa-mānakūṭā appaṭi-
viratā ║ la ║ ║

(5) Ukkoṭana.
4. Evam eva kho bhikkhave appakā te sattā ye ukkoṭa-
navañcananikatisāviyogā2 paṭiviratā ║ atha kho ete va ba-
hutarā sattā ye ukkoṭanavañcananikatisāviyogā2 appati-
visatā ║ la ║ ║

--101. (6--11) Vadha-ālopa-sāhasakāram.
4. Evam eva kho bhikkhave appakā te sattā ye che-
dana-vadha-bandhana-viparāmosa-ālopa-sāhasākārā3 pati-

CHAPTER XI. GATIYO PAÑCAKĀ.

(1) Pañcagati.
2. Atha kho Bhagavā parittam nakhasikhāyam paṃsum
āropetvā bhikkhū āmantesi ║ ║
3. Taṃ kim maññatha bhikkhave ║ katamaṃ nu kho
bahutaraṃ yo vāyam mayā paritto nakhasikhāyam2 paṃsu3
āropito4 ║ ayaṃ vā mahāpathavī ti ║ ║
Etad eva bhante bahutaraṃ yad idam mahāpathavī
appamattako5 yam Bhagavatā paritto nakhasikhāyam
paṃsu3 āropito saṅkham pi na upeti upanidham6 pi na
upeti kalabhāgam pi na upeti mahāpathavim upanidhāya7
Bhagavatā paritto nakhasikhāyam paṃsu3 āropito ti ║ ║
4. Evam eva kho bhikkhave appakā te sattā ye ma-
nussā cutā manussesu paccājāyanti ║ atha kho ete va ba-
hutarā sattā ye manussā cutā niraye paccājāyanti ║ la-pe ║ ║

(2) Pañcagati.
4. Evam eva kho bhikkhave appakā te sattā ye manussā
cutā manussesu paccājāyanti ║ atha kho ete va bahutarā
sattā ye manussā cutā tiracchānayoniyā paccājāyanti ║ ║

--107. (4--6) Pañcagati.

tiracchānayoniyā paccājāyanti ║
pittivisaye paccājāyanti ║ ║

--110. (7--9) Pañcagati.

devā cutā niraye paccājāyanti ║

pittivisaye paccājāyanti ║ ║

(30) Pañcagati.

catunnam ║ ║
